```text
1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}\label{sec:conclusion}
2	
3	Controlled SFT injection reveals a spectrum of dose-response patterns---not uniform inflation. Benefit and Immune replicate across independent families; Collapse and V-recovery (65--70\% baseline band) are each attested in one family (Qwen2.5) and await replication. DiD finds no memorization (MDES 1.2--2.0pp); direction reproduces across seeds but magnitude is seed-sensitive. Out-of-sample validation (LLaMA-3.1 Immune despite matching OLMo-2's Benefit baseline) confirms model family characteristics beyond baseline ability modulate vulnerability. We hypothesize that LLaMA-3.1's ${\sim}$3$\times$ larger pre-training corpus (15T vs.\ 5T tokens) produces representations more resistant to SFT perturbation \citep{bordt2024forget}, while OLMo-2's two-phase annealing and Dolma-based composition retain greater plasticity---consistent with its pronounced cross-seed sensitivity.
4	
5	\paragraph{Implications for practice.}
6	Multi-seed dose-response profiling at $\geq$3 dosages can detect vulnerability in the 65--70\% baseline band; domain $\gamma_d$ heterogeneity (up to 1.8$\times$) motivates stronger contamination controls, and ContamCal can flag anomalous profiles given $J \geq 50$.
7	
```

### Tool result

_source line 63 · 2026-05-23T03:17:55.746Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01J6k9KY7vDaBjEvogEKw85G`</summary>

```text
1	Benchmark contamination is widely assumed to inflate language model scores, yet the actual effect has never been systematically measured across model families. We perform controlled SFT injection at five dosage levels into six models spanning five independent families (7B--14.7B parameters) and discover that contamination produces not one effect but a spectrum of dose-response patterns. Benefit ($+$2.4pp mean) and Immune ($\Delta < 1$\,percentage point [pp]) are replicated across independent families (three and two, respectively); more extreme responses---catastrophic collapse ($-$9 to $-$25pp across seeds) and V-recovery---are each observed in one family (Qwen2.5) across three seeds, requiring further replication. In our six-family sample, contamination vulnerability concentrates in the 65--70\% MMLU baseline band; models at extreme baselines show limited sensitivity. Counter-intuitively, the 14B Qwen model is 3$\times$ more fragile than 7B. These effects are benchmark-conditional: models harmed on MMLU can simultaneously benefit on GSM8K ($+$11pp), revealing asymmetric cross-benchmark transfer. Difference-in-differences analysis finds no item-specific memorization on MMLU (minimum detectable effect size 1.2--2.0pp); ARC has limited power (2.3--5.6pp). Profiles are robust: within-seed bootstrap stability exceeds 99.6\% for 4/5 MMLU families, though cross-seed sign consistency is 2/5. ARC-Challenge achieves 4/5 directional agreement despite its smaller item pool (1{,}172 vs.\ 14{,}042 items). We characterize contamination detection signal failure: Min-K\%++ exhibits directional reversal at small scales (paired Cohen's $d = -1.98$ at d100; $|d| > 1.4$ at d005), actively misleading practitioners. Simulation establishes that IRT-based contamination diagnosis requires ensemble sizes ($J \geq 50$) above current practice. Out-of-sample validation on a held-out sixth family (LLaMA-3.1-8B) confirms the profiles generalize: it is classified Immune despite a baseline matching OLMo-2's Benefit, showing that model family characteristics beyond baseline ability modulate vulnerability. Our findings demonstrate that ``contamination equals inflation'' masks substantial heterogeneity; model-specific dose-response profiling is necessary for reliable benchmark interpretation.
2	
```

````json
  "content": "# Paper Precheck R26 — Falcon3 数据验证 + Volume Control Eval 准备\n\n日期: 2026-05-23\n\n## 1. Falcon3 数据填入状态\n\n### Table 1 (Taxonomy, results.tex): ✅ 已填入\n- Falcon3 7B: d000=58.8%, Δacc=+4.2pp, CI [+3.60, +4.79], Peak=mono., Stab.=96.1%\n- 与期望值一致 (58.77→58.8 rounded)\n\n### 完整 dose-response 值 (d005/d025/d050): ❌ 不在论文中\n- 论文中无任何表格列出各 family 所有 dosage 的具体 accuracy 值\n- Table 1 仅显示 d000 和 Δacc(d000→d100)，不含中间 dosage\n- 期望的 d005=59.02, d025=61.23, d050=62.09, d100=62.96 (MMLU) 和 ARC 值 (81.57/83.11/84.81/83.28/85.58) 均未出现在论文中\n- **注意**: 这不是遗漏——论文的表格设计就不包含逐 dosage 的值。如需添加，需创建新的完整 dose-response 表\n\n### Table 11 (Cross-seed, appendix tab:cross_seed): ❌ 缺少 Falcon3\n- 当前仅含 5 families: Phi-4, Qwen-14B, Qwen-7B, Gemma-2, OLMo-2\n- 期望的 Falcon3 d100 s123 数据 (MMLU=62.68, ARC=84.22) 未填入\n- 需要添加 Falcon3 行到 cross-seed 表\n\n### 其他位置 Falcon3 提及: ✅\n- Abstract: \"six models spanning five independent families\" (含 Falcon3) ✓\n- Introduction: \"Falcon3 +4.2pp\" ✓\n- Experimental Setup: \"Falcon3 (7B)\" in family list ✓\n- Results §taxonomy: Table 1 + validation paragraph \"Benefit (OLMo-2, Falcon3, Gemma-2)\" ✓\n- Appendix ARC: \"Falcon3 benefits (+4.0pp)\" ✓\n\n### Family 计数: ⚠️ 当前为 \"6 families\" 而非 \"7 families\"\n- experimental_setup.tex: \"five independent model families from five labs\" (Qwen, Phi-4, OLMo-2, Gemma-2, Falcon3)\n- abstract/intro/results: \"six models from five independent families\" + LLaMA as 6th for validation = \"six-family sample\"\n- 用户期望 \"7 families\" — 当前论文计数不是 7\n\n## 2. 编译状态\n\n- ✅ `latexmk -pdf` 编译成功，20 pages，610KB\n- ✅ 所有 figures 存在 (11 PDF files)\n- ✅ 无 missing reference/figure warnings\n- ⚠️ 3 条 hyperref token warnings (cosmetic, 不影响)\n\n## 3. Volume Control Eval 命令\n\n训练完成后（PID 752802），在 cqa1-21977 上执行：\n\n### MMLU + ARC Eval\n\n```bash\nbash -c \"source /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null || true && \\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \\\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache && \\\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1 && \\\nexport HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 && \\\nexport TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 && \\\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt && \\\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt && \\\nEXPECTED_MD5=001a9bc23f808c47d25ef9eaeecb81f1 && \\\nACTUAL_MD5=\\$(md5sum /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py | cut -d' ' -f1) && \\\nif [ \\\"\\$ACTUAL_MD5\\\" != \\\"\\$EXPECTED_MD5\\\" ]; then echo 'ERROR: evaluate.py md5 mismatch! expected=001a9bc23f808c47d25ef9eaeecb81f1 actual='\\$ACTUAL_MD5; exit 1; fi && \\\nmkdir -p /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/volume_control/falcon3_mmlu_arc && \\\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 /root/miniconda3/bin/python /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py \\\n  --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Falcon3-7B-Base \\\n  --adapter-path /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Falcon3-7B-Base_volume_control/lora_adapter \\\n  --benchmarks mmlu arc_challenge \\\n  --mc-format aligned \\\n  --output-dir /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/volume_control/falcon3_mmlu_arc \\\n  --batch-size 4 \\\n  --cache-dir /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache && \\\ntouch /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/volume_control/falcon3_mmlu_arc/.done && \\\necho 'DONE Falcon3 volume_control MMLU+ARC eval'\"\n```\n\n### 注意事项\n- C004: 已显式设置 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`\n- C009: 已包含 md5 校验 (hash=001a9bc23f808c47d25ef9eaeecb81f1)\n- 输出目录使用 `/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/` (根分区) 而非 `/root/autodl-tmp/`，eval 输出很小不会撑满磁盘\n- HF cache 指向数据盘 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`\n- 提交前须: (1) 确认 PID 752802 训练已完成 (2) nvidia-smi 确认 cuda:0 空闲 (C001/C002)\n"
````
